BARAMATI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, BARAMATI
Final Reservation Notification
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1926/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Baramati Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Baramati Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification
was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification the suggestions and
objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby notify the final reservation for the
purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Baramati Municipal Council, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

FURSUNGI-URULI DEVACHI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, FURSUNGI
Final Reservation Notification
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1926/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Fursungi-Uruli
Devachi Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Fursungi-Uruli Devachi Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft
reservation notification was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification
the suggestions and objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14th October 2025.

Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby notify the final reservation for the
purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Fursungi-Uruli Devachi

LONAVALA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, LONAVALA
Final Reservation Notification
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1926/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Lonavala Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of

the Lonavala Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification
was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification the suggestions and
objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby notify the final reservation for the
purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Lonavala Municipal Council, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

TALEGAON-DABHADE MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, TALEGAON
Final Reservation Notification
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1926/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Talegaon-Dabhade
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Talegaon-Dabhade Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation
notification was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification the
suggestions and objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby notify the final reservation for the
purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Talegaon-Dabhade Municipal
Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

JEJURI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, JEJURI
Final Reservation Notification
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1926/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Jejuri Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Jejuri Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification
was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification the suggestions and
objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14th October 2025.

Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby notify the final reservation for the
purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Jejuri Municipal Council, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

BHOR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, BHOR
Final Reservation Notification
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1926/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Bhor Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Bhor Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification
was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification the suggestions and
objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby notify the final reservation for the
purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Bhor Municipal Council, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

SHIRUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SHIRUR
Final Reservation Notification
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1926/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the shirur Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Shirur Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification
was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification the suggestions and
objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby notify the final reservation for the
purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Shirur Municipal Council, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1926/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-

whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Alandi Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of

was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional

Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the

purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Alandi Municipal Council, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Vadgaon
Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Vadgaon Nagarpanchayat was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification
was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification the suggestions and
objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14th October 2025.

Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby notify the final reservation for the
purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Vadgaon Nagarpanchayat, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

Final Reservation Notification
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1926/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Malegaon bk
Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Malegaon bk Nagarpanchayat was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation
notification was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification the
suggestions and objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14th October 2025.

Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby notify the final reservation for the
purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Malegaon bk Nagarpanchayat,
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

MANCHAR NAGARPANCHAYAT, MANCHAR
Final Reservation Notification
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1926/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Manchar
Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Manchar Nagarpanchayat was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification
was published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification the suggestions and
objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby notify the final reservation for the
purpose of the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Manchar Nagarpanchayat, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

VADGAON MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, VADGAON
Final Reservation Notification
No. Desk.11/NP/ ElectionSR/ Sank.7/2025-26/168/2025.--Hon. State Election
Commission Order : Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order
No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Vadgaon Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation
draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the
reservation draw for the wards of the Vadgaon Municipal Council was conducted and published
on 08th October 2025.
And whereas, The draft of the said reservation was notified in the Gazette dated
09/10/2025 and suggestions and objections were invited till 14/10/2025. Accordingly, the Chief
Officer of Vadgaon Municipal Council has not received any suggestions or objections regarding
the draft of the said reservation. The Chief Officer of Vadgaon Municipal Council has informed
that.
And whereas, Hon. Divisional Commissioner, Pune vide order No. 609/2025 Dated 24
/10/2025 has given final approval for the reservation draw by the Vadgaon Municipal Council.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the final
proposal of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Vadgaon
Municipal Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One - A - Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025 as follows.

GADHINGLAJ MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, GADHINGLAJ
Final Reservation Notification
No. Desk.11/NP/ ElectionSR/ Sank.7/2025-26/168/2025.--Hon. State Election
Commission Order : Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order
No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Gadhinglaj Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation
draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the
reservation draw for the wards of the Gadhinglaj Municipal Council was conducted and
published on 08th October 2025.
And whereas, The draft of the said reservation was notified in the Gazette dated
09/10/2025 and suggestions and objections were invited till 14/10/2025. Accordingly, the Chief
Officer of Gadhinglaj Municipal Council has not received any suggestions or objections
regarding the draft of the said reservation. The Chief Officer of Gadhinglaj Municipal Council
has informed that.
And whereas, Hon. Divisional Commissioner, Pune vide order No. 609/2025 Dated
24 /10/2025 has given final approval for the reservation draw by the Gadhinglaj Municipal
Council.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the final
proposal of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the
Gadhinglaj Municipal Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One - A - Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025 as
follows.

HUPARI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, HUPARI
Final Reservation Notification
No. Desk.11/NP/ ElectionSR/ Sank.7/2025-26/168/2025.--Hon. State Election
Commission Order : Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order
No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Hupari
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation
draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the
reservation draw for the wards of the Hupari Municipal Council was conducted and published on
08th October 2025.
And whereas, The draft of the said reservation was notified in the Gazette dated
09/10/2025 and suggestions and objections were invited till 14/10/2025. Accordingly, the Chief
Officer of Hupari Municipal Council has not received any suggestions or objections regarding
the draft of the said reservation. The Chief Officer of Hupari Municipal Council has informed
that.
And whereas, Hon. Divisional Commissioner, Pune vide order No. 609/2025 Dated
24 /10/2025 has given final approval for the reservation draw by the Hupari Municipal Council.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the final
proposal of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Hupari
Municipal Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One - A - Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025 as follows.

PANHALA HILL STATION MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, PANHALA
Final Reservation Notification
No. Desk.11/NP/ ElectionSR/ Sank.7/2025-26/168/2025.--Hon. State Election
Commission Order : Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order
No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Panhala Hill Station Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra
Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation
draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the
reservation draw for the wards of the Panhala Hill Station Municipal Council was conducted and
published on 08th October 2025.
And whereas, The draft of the said reservation was notified in the Gazette dated
09/10/2025 and suggestions and objections were invited till 14/10/2025. Accordingly, the Chief
Officer of Panhala Hill Station Municipal Council has not received any suggestions or objections
regarding the draft of the said reservation. The Chief Officer of Panhala Hill Station Municipal
Council has informed that.
And whereas, Hon. Divisional Commissioner, Pune vide order No. 609/2025 Dated
24/10/2025 has given final approval for the reservation draw by the Panhala Hill Station
Municipal Council.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the final
proposal of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Panhala
Hill Station Municipal Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One - A - Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025 as
follows.

Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated

Malkapur Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation
draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the
reservation draw for the wards of the Malkapur Municipal Council was conducted and published
on 08th October 2025.
And whereas, The draft of the said reservation was notified in the Gazette dated
09/10/2025 and suggestions and objections were invited till 14/10/2025. Accordingly, the Chief
Officer of Malkapur Municipal Council has not received any suggestions or objections regarding
the draft of the said reservation. The Chief Officer of Malkapur Municipal Council has informed
that.
And whereas, Hon. Divisional Commissioner, Pune vide order No. 609/2025 Dated
24/10/2025 has given final approval for the reservation draw by the Malkapur Municipal Council.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested

order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the final

JAYSINGPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, JAYSINGPUR
Final Reservation Notification
No. Desk.11/NP/ ElectionSR/ Sank.7/2025-26/168/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission
Order : Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Jaysingpur Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-
6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been
delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw
for the wards of the Jaysingpur Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October
2025.
And whereas, The draft of the said reservation was notified in the Gazette dated
09/10/2025 and suggestions and objections were invited till 14/10/2025. Accordingly, the Chief
Officer of Jaysingpur Municipal Council has not received any suggestions or objections regarding
the draft of the said reservation. The Chief Officer of Jaysingpur Municipal Council has informed
that.
And whereas, Hon. Divisional Commissioner, Pune vide order No. 609/2025 Dated 24
/10/2025 has given final approval for the reservation draw by the Jaysingpur Municipal Council.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested in
me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the final proposal
of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Jaysingpur
Municipal Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One
- A - Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025 as follows.

KURUNDWAD MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, KURUNDWAD
Final Reservation Notification
No. Desk.11/NP/ ElectionSR/ Sank.7/2025-26/168/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission
Order : Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Kurundwad Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been
delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for
the wards of the Kurundwad Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
And whereas, The draft of the said reservation was notified in the Gazette dated 09/10/2025
and suggestions and objections were invited till 14/10/2025. Accordingly, the Chief Officer of
Kurundwad Municipal Council has not received any suggestions or objections regarding the draft of
the said reservation. The Chief Officer of Kurundwad Municipal Council has informed that.
And whereas, Hon. Divisional Commissioner, Pune vide order No. 609/2025 Dated
24 /10/2025 has given final approval for the reservation draw by the Kurundwad Municipal Council.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested in
me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order
of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the final proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Kurundwad Municipal
Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One - A - Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025 as follows.

HATKANANGALE NAGARPANCHAYAT, HATKANANGALE
Final Reservation Notification
No. Desk.11/NP/ ElectionSR/ Sank.7/2025-26/168/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission
Order : Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Hatkanangale Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been
delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for
the wards of the Hatkanangale Nagarpanchayat was conducted and published on 08th October
2025.
And whereas, The draft of the said reservation was notified in the Gazette dated 09/10/2025
and suggestions and objections were invited till 14/10/2025. Accordingly, the Chief Officer of
Hatkanangale Nagarpanchayat has not received any suggestions or objections regarding the draft
of the said reservation. The Chief Officer of Hatkanangale Nagarpanchayat has informed that.
And whereas, Hon. Divisional Commissioner, Pune vide order No. 609/2025 Dated
24 /10/2025 has given final approval for the reservation draw by the Hatkanangale
Nagarpanchayat.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested in
me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order
of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the final proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Hatkanangale
Nagarpanchayat, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One - A
- Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025 as follows.

SATARA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SATARA
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-80/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Orders: Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Dated
6th October 2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, the
final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Satara Municipal
Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October
2025, RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, dated 6th October 2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Satara
Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of
Satara Municipal Council was to receive citizens’ objections/suggestions from 9th October 2025 to 14th
October 2025. As per the Hon. State Election Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District
Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise reservation in the Government Gazette after
considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft reservation.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Satara Municipal Council as
follows:

KARAD MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, KARAD
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-80/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Orders: Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Dated
6th October 2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, the
final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Karad Municipal
Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October
2025, RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, dated 6th October 2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Karad Municipal
Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then published in
the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated
09th October 2025.In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of Karad Municipal Council
was to receive citizens’ objections/suggestions from 9th October 2025 to 14th October 2025. As per the
Hon. State Election Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to
publish the final ward-wise reservation in the Government Gazette after considering the
objections/suggestions received on the draft reservation.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Karad Municipal Council as follows:

PHALTAN MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, PHALTAN
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-80/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Orders: Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Dated
6th October 2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, the
final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Phaltan Municipal
Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October
2025, RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, dated 6th October 2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Phaltan
Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025.In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of
Phaltan Municipal Council was to receive citizens’ objections/suggestions from 9th October 2025 to 14th
October 2025. As per the Hon. State Election Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District
Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise reservation in the Government Gazette after
considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft reservation.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Phaltan Municipal Council as
follows:

WAI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, WAI
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-80/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Orders: Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Dated
6th October 2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, the
final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Wai Municipal
Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October
2025, RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, dated 6th October 2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Wai Municipal
Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then published in
the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated
09th October 2025.In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of Wai Municipal Council
was to receive citizens’ objections/suggestions from 9th October 2025 to 14th October 2025. As per the
Hon. State Election Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to
publish the final ward-wise reservation in the Government Gazette after considering the
objections/suggestions received on the draft reservation.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Wai Municipal Council as follows:

Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-80/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Orders: Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Dated
6th October 2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, the
final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Panchgani Hill
Station Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A
– Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October
2025, RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, dated 6th October 2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Panchgani Hill
Station Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was
then published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025.In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of
Panchgani Hill Station Municipal Council was to receive citizens’ objections/suggestions from 9th
October 2025 to 14th October 2025. As per the Hon. State Election Commission’s order dated
03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise reservation in the
Government Gazette after considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft reservation.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Panchgani Hill Station Municipal

MAHABALESHWAR HILLSTATION MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, MAHABALESHWAR
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-80/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Orders: Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Dated
6th October 2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, the
final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Mahabaleshwar
Hillstation Municipal Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October
2025, RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, dated 6th October 2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Mahabaleshwar
Hillstation Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification
was then published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 09th October 2025.In accordance with the said notification, the Chief
Officer of Mahabaleshwar Hillstation Municipal Council was to receive citizens’ objections/suggestions
from 9th October 2025 to 14th October 2025. As per the Hon. State Election Commission’s order dated
03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise reservation in the
Government Gazette after considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft reservation.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Mahabaleshwar Hillstation
Municipal Council as follows:

MALKAPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, MALKAPUR
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-80/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Orders: Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Dated
6th October 2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, the
final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Malkapur Municipal
Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October
2025, RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 3rd October 2025, dated 6th October 2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Malkapur
Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 09th October 2025.In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of
Malkapur Municipal Council was to receive citizens’ objections/suggestions from 9th October 2025 to
14th October 2025. As per the Hon. State Election Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District
Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise reservation in the Government Gazette after
considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft reservation.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Malkapur Municipal Council as
follows:

ISLAMPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, ISLAMPUR
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi- 604/2025.---- Hon. State Election Commission Orders : Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and dated 06/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Islampur Municipal
Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, dated 06/10/2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Islampur Municipal Council was
conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then published in the Maharashtra
Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 09th October 2025.
In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of Islampur Municipal Council yat was to receive
citizens’ objections/suggestions from 09/10/2025 to 14/10/2025. As per the Hon. State Election
Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise
reservation in the Government Gazette after considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft
reservation.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade (I.A.S.), District Collector, Sangli, by exercising the powers vested in
me by the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the
order issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Islampur Municipal Council as
follows :-

No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi- 604/2025.----- Hon. State Election Commission Orders : Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, Order No.RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and dated 06/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Vita Municipal
Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, dated 06/10/2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Vita Municipal Council was
conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then published in the Maharashtra
Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 09th October
2025.In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of Vita Municipal Council was to receive
citizens’ objections/suggestions from 09/10/2025 to 14/10/2025. As per the Hon. State Election
Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise
reservation in the Government Gazette after considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft
reservation.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Vita Municipal Council as follows :

ASHOK KAKADE (I.A.S.),
Collector, Sangli.
Sangli, 24th October 2025.

ASHTA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, ASHTA
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi- 604/2025.---- Hon. State Election Commission Orders : Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and dated 06/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Ashta Municipal
Council / Nagar Panchayat have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary
Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, dated 06/10/2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Ashta Municipal Council / Nagar
Panchayat was conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then published in
the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated
09th October 2025. In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of Ashta Municipal Council /
Nagar Panchayat was to receive citizens’ objections/suggestions from 09/10/2025 to 14/10/2025. As per
the Hon. State Election Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to
publish the final ward-wise reservation in the Government Gazette after considering the objections/
suggestions received on the draft reservation.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Ashta Municipal Council / Nagar
Panchayat as follows:

TASGAON MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, TASGAON
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-604/2025.----Hon. State Election Commission Orders : Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and dated 06/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Tasgaon Municipal
Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, dated 06/10/2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Tasgaon Municipal Council was
conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then published in the Maharashtra
Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 09th October 2025.
In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of Tasgaon Municipal Council was to receive
citizens’ objections/suggestions from 09/10/2025 to 14/10/2025. As per the Hon. State Election
Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise
reservation in the Government Gazette after considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft
reservation.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Tasgaon Municipal Council as
follows :

PALUS MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, PALUS
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-604/2025.---- Hon. State Election Commission Orders : Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and dated 06/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Palus Municipal
Council have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, dated 06/10/2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Palus Municipal Council was
conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then published in the Maharashtra
Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 09th October 2025.
In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of Palus Municipal Council was to receive
citizens’ objections/suggestions from 09/10/2025 to 14/10/2025. As per the Hon. State Election
Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise
reservation in the Government Gazette after considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft
reservation.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the Draft Reservation Notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Palus Municipal Council as follows :

No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-604/2025.---- Hon. State Election Commission Orders : Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and dated 06/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Shirala
Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A
– Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 3/10/2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3/10/2025,
dated 06/10/2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Shirala Nagar Panchayat was conducted on 08th

accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of Shirala Nagar Panchayat was to receive citizens’
objections/suggestions from 09/10/2025 to 14/10/2025. As per the Hon. State Election Commission’s
order dated 03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise reservation in
the Government Gazette after considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft reservation.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order

ATPADI NAGARPANCHAYAT, ATPADI
Final Reservation Notification
No. NP/Election/Karya-5/Kavi-604/2025.---- Hon. State Election Commission Orders : Order No.
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and dated 06/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries and ward numbers for the upcoming elections of the Atpadi Nagar
Panchayat have been published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, under the powers conferred to the respective District Collectors by the Hon. State
Election Commission through its orders Order No. RaNia/NP-2025/ Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.10(b)/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and RaNia/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, dated 06/10/2025 a draw for reservation of wards for the Atpadi Nagar Panchayat was
conducted on 08th October 2025. The draft reservation notification was then published in the Maharashtra
Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 09th October 2025.
In accordance with the said notification, the Chief Officer of Atpadi Nagar Panchayat was to receive
citizens’ objections/suggestions from 09/10/2025 to 14/10/2025. As per the Hon. State Election
Commission’s order dated 03/10/2025, the District Collector was instructed to publish the final ward-wise
reservation in the Government Gazette after considering the objections/suggestions received on the draft
reservation.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, by exercising the powers vested in me by
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and in accordance with the order
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, and as per the final ward structure
published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025, and after due consideration of the objections and suggestions
received on the draft reservation notification dated 09/10/2025, do hereby notify the final ward-wise
reservation for the purpose of the upcoming general elections of the Atpadi Nagar Panchayat as follows :

Pr. No. 10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated

whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of Wards for the upcoming elections of the Barshi Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the Wards of
the Barshi Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025. Accordingly, vide its
letter No. NAVI-7/KAVI 857 dated 08th October 2025, the notification of reservation draw of Barshi
Municipal Council was published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette dated 09th October 2025 and
objections and suggestions were invited thereon one objections or suggestions was received within the
prescribed time limit on 14th October 2025 till 3.00 pm.and It has been rejected.
therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad (I.A.S.), District Collector, Solapur , in exercise of the powers
vested in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal
of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Barshi Municipal Council
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

PANDHARPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCILPANDHARPUR
Final Reservation Notification

No. NAVI-7/KAVI/ 881 /2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP 2025/Pr.
No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and LetterNo. RANIA/NP-2025/ Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03rd
October 2025
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Pandharpur
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the right to publish the final notification for reservation have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Pandharpur Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025 and suggestions and
objections were invited from 09th October 2025 to 14 th October 2025.
therefore, after carefully considering the suggestions and objections received, I, Kumar
Ashirwad, District Collector, Solapur, in exercise of the powers vested in me under the above-
mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the Urban
Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the following reservation for the
upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Pandharpur Municipal Council as notified in
the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement,
dated 29th September 2025.

No. NAVI 7/KAVI-881/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.
No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and LetterNo. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03rd
October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Akkalkot Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03rd
October 2025, the powers to publish a Final notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Akkalkot Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad I.A.S. District Collector, Solapur , in exercise of the powers
vested in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the Final proposal
of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Akkalkot Municipal

No.10/Ka-6, date 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No. 11/Ka-6, dated 03rd
October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Kurduwadi
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A - Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11 /Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Kurduwadi Municipal Council at was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad I.A.S.District Collector, Solapur, in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order
of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of

reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Kurduwadi Municipal

Final Reservation Notification
No. NAVI7/ KAVI.-881/2025.-- Hon.State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.
No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/ Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Karmala Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Karmala Municipal Council was conducted on 08th October 2025. Accordingly, this office’s letter
No.Navi 7/kawi 857/2025 dated 08th October 2025, the notification of reservation draw of Karmala
Municipal Council was published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette dated 09th October 2025
and objections and suggestions were invited thereon. No. objection or suggestion was received within
the time limit stipulated in this regard i.e. up to 3.00 pm on 14th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad I.A.S.District Collector, Solapur , in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order
of the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Karmala Municipal Council,
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

SANGOLA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SANGOLA
Final Reservation Notification
No. NAVI7/ KAVI. 881/2025.--- Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.
No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Sangola Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the powers to publish a Final notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Sangola Municipal Council was conducted On 08th October 2025. Accordingly, this office letter no.
Navi7/Kawi 857/2025 dated 08th October 2025, the notification of reservation draw of Sangola
Municipal Council was published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette dated 09th October 2025 and
objections and suggestions were invited their on no objection or suggestion was received within the
time limit stipulated in this regard, i.e. up to 3.00 pm on 14th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad I. A.S., District Collector, Solapur, in exercise of the powers
vested in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the Final proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Sangola Municipal Council
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
