AKLUJ MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, AKLUJ
Final Reservation Notification
No. NAVI7/ KAVI./881/2025.-- Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.
No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/ Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03rd
October 2025.
whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June
2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Akluj Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd October 2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Akluj Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025. Accordingly, this
office's letter No. Navi 7/Kawi 857/2025 dated 08th October 2025, the notification of reservation draw of
Akluj Municipal Council was published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette dated 09th October
2025 and objections and suggestions were invited thereon. No objection or suggestion was received
within the time limit stipulated in this regard, i.e. up to 3.00 pm on 14th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad I.A.S.District Collector, Solapur , in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order
of the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the final proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Akluj Municipal Council , as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

Final Reservation Notification
No. NAVI7/KAVI-881/2025.---- Hon. State Election Commission : Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3/10/2025, and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
3/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Mangalwedha
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per
the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of the Mangalwedha
Municipal Council it was conducted and published on 08th October 2025. Accordingly, this office's
letter No. Navi 7/Kawi 857/2025, dated 08/10/2025, the notification of reservation draw of
Mangalwedha Municipal Council was published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette dated
09/10/2025 and objections and suggestions were invited thereon. No objection or suggestion was
received within the time limit stipulated in this regard, i.e. up to 3.00 pm on 14/10/2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad (I.A.S.), District Collector, Solapur, in exercise of the powers
vested in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the final proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Mangalwedha Municipal
Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.

letter No. Navi 7/Kawi 857/2025 dated 08/10/2025, the notification of reservation draw of Angar
Nagarpanchayat was published in the Maharashtra Government Gazette dated 09/10/2025 and
objections and suggestions were invited thereon. No objection or suggestion was received within the
time limit stipulated in this regard, i.e. up to 3.00 pm on 14/10/2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad I.A.S.District Collector, Solapur , in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order
of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the Final proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Angar Nagarpanchayat, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
